# Design prompts — ready to paste into ChatGPT

One block per static graphic a content idea actually needs, grouped by where it's posted. Each prompt already has the exact size, the joke's on-screen text, brand colours, typography style and logo rule baked in from the idea's design brief — copy the whole fenced block and paste it into an image generator as-is.

Video-only formats (the Reel/TikTok cut itself) aren't here — this is for the static posters and cover frames, which is what an image generator actually produces. Full shot lists, audio direction and duration for filming stay in each channel's own content file (reels.md, video-ideas.md, posts.md).

## Instagram feed post (4:5) — 1080x1350px

### X-001 — Loyalty is a hostage situation

**Size:** 1080x1350px

**Paste into ChatGPT / DALL-E:**

```
Create a 1080x1350px social graphic for Instagram feed post (4:5). UK English. Europe/London audience.

BRAND: BroadbandPicker, independent UK broadband comparison. Rankings are not sold.

REFERENCE SYSTEM (copy this layout, then change the joke):
Match the BroadbandPicker poster system (the Engineer Window reference). 1) Full-bleed outer canvas in deep navy #0F172A. 2) A large rounded paper card #F8FAFC (corner radius 28-32px) sitting on the navy so a navy footer strip remains at the bottom. 3) Top of the card: one small sky-blue #0EA5E9 icon, centred (clock, wifi arc, kettle). 4) Headline in huge Inter ExtraBold navy #0F172A, 2 to 5 words, slightly tracked, British English. 5) A thin sky #0EA5E9 hairline under the headline. 6) One giant navy fact, time or price (example: 8AM-6PM). 7) A sky-blue sarcastic punchline under the fact, smaller than the headline (example: So... Tuesday.). 8) Illustration: clean editorial British cartoon, black/navy outlines, flat colour, limited palette navy/sky/grey/white. Split before/after or left/right panels with tiny navy time labels. Simple cartoon people (jumpers, slippers, glasses) not photoreal, not stock, not 3D, not celebrity. British terrace, kettle, mug, router, van, flask, pizza box, laptop allowed. 9) Navy footer: left-aligned sky rounded-square wifi-mark (two white arcs + white dot, like public/logo.svg) then white wordmark BroadbandPicker and URL broadbandpicker.co.uk in smaller white or sky type. 10) Matte. One joke. No extra slogans, no QR, no fake ISP logos, no SALE red, no emojis on the artwork, no 'comment below'.

CROP FOR THIS SIZE: Portrait poster stacked like the Engineer Window reference: icon, headline, giant fact, sky punchline, then the cartoon, then the navy logo footer.

COLOURS: navy #0F172A, sky #0EA5E9, paper #F8FAFC, white #FFFFFF. Green #22C55E only as a single tick. Palette: Outer navy #0F172A, inner paper card #F8FAFC, headline navy, icon and punchline sky #0EA5E9, wordmark white on the navy footer, green #22C55E only if the joke is genuine good news (one tick). Illustration greys plus sky jumpers. No rainbow, no neon, no SALE red.

TYPE: Inter ExtraBold for the headline, Inter Regular for the whisper. Anton or Impact for the joke; Inter Bold for the end card.

LOGO: Footer only. Sky rounded-square wifi-mark (#0EA5E9, two white arcs + white dot, public/logo.svg) on the navy strip, left, with white wordmark BroadbandPicker and broadbandpicker.co.uk. Never recolour the mark green. Never put it on the punchline. Never a generic stock wifi icon. Never a QR code.

PEOPLE: Simple editorial cartoon people allowed (jumpers, slippers, glasses). Not photoreal, not stock smiles, not 3D.

THIS JOKE: Two fake bills side by side. Left labelled NEW CUSTOMER in sky blue. Right labelled LOYAL CUSTOMER in angry red-navy. Same tiny print energy as a real ISP email.

ON-SCREEN TEXT, exactly, large, no extras: "New customer: £19" | "You: £41" | "That's not loyalty." | "That's a hostage situation."

AVOID: Don't use a real customer's bill; Don't name a fake official tariff. No fake BT/Sky/Virgin logos. No QR. No SALE red. No watermarks. No 'comment below'. Fibre not fiber. The caption, not the artwork, asks the question.
```
